# Crusader Dolls — arms for the bookmark's characters (docs/heraldry.md). On the bookmark screen, the banner beside each
# name is the bookmark title's arms (a landless adventurer's: her house's); the selected character's panel shows her
# house's or dynasty's. Arms the game generates in play do not exist there, and show black.

# Gotland (c_gutland), Steinn's county and the Bædling seat, and Gute (dynasty 825), his house: a red Gotland whirl on
# blue. Replaces vanilla's arms for Gotland and its Norse variant (c_gutland_norse, set at game start and for Norse
# holders), at the user's choice, so the map banner in the character selector shows it. The whirl is the disc of
# Gotland's picture stones; blue with red is the Fyrkat vǫlva's dress (blue wool, red cords, c. 980). Never black on
# white or white on black, which reads as the "Black Sun".
c_gutland = {
	pattern = "pattern_solid.dds"
	color1 = "blue"
	colored_emblem = {
		texture = "ce_gotland_spiral.dds"
		color1 = "red"
		instance = { position = { 0.5 0.5 } scale = { 1.28 1.28 } }	# the whirl fills 70% of its texture: 1.28 makes it 90% of the field
	}
}

# The Mother House (d_tm_mother_house), Theia Beatrike's title, Adelphai Dorleyaiou: a gold falcon on a white roundel,
# on purple. The user's design. Matar's most frequent attribute in central Phrygia is the hawk or falcon (Roller 1999,
# p. 108). From the user's image by tools/img2coa.py at 200 px with --disc: the roundel is vanilla ce_circle, the
# falcon 563 rectangles of ce_block_02.
